- **lc-msms-chromatogram-simulation**: Use when the goal is to generate a gold-standard synthetic mzML for LC-MS/MS (peptides, nucleosides, or lipids defined by chemical formulas) and benchmark it against a real run, e.g. reproducing a 140-min proteomics gradient simulation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lc-msms-chromatogram-simulation))
- **lc-msms-consensus-spectra-processing**: Use when a multi-sample LC-MS/MS untargeted metabolomics data set (e.g. MSV000080502 Euphorbia dendroides extract) must be turned into clean consensus spectra with ionization variants (in-source fragments, adducts, dimers) merged into one representative [M+H]+ ion. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lc-msms-consensus-spectra-processing))
- **lc-msms-dda-run-simulation**: Use when a gold-standard LC-MS/MS dataset with known ground truth is needed to benchmark peptide identification pipelines, test search/rescoring algorithms, or predict co-elution and co-fragmentation before running real MS experiments. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lc-msms-dda-run-simulation))
- **lc-msms-feature-alignment**: Use when aligning per-sample precursor (MS1) and fragment (MS2) m/z/intensity feature lists exported from MS-DIAL across many LC-MS/MS samples into one feature table for downstream multivariate or machine-learning analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lc-msms-feature-alignment))
- **lc-msms-feature-annotation-msi-level3**: Use when mzML files and OpenMS-annotated featureXML adduct information are available and you need to attach SIRIUS molecular formula and CSI:FingerID structural identifications (MSI level 3) to detected LC-MS/MS features. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lc-msms-feature-annotation-msi-level3))
- **lc-msms-feature-based-molecular-networking-processing**: Use when the analysis goal is to detect, align, quantify and dereplicate LC-MS/MS features from natural product extract fractions in order to generate an MS/MS-annotated feature table for downstream bioactivity-correlation mapping (e.g., antiviral screening). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lc-msms-feature-based-molecular-networking-processing))
- **lc-msms-feature-count-validation**: Use when running the specXplore importing pipeline on .mgf MS/MS feature lists (e.g. wheat or urine exposome data) and you need a scale sanity check — wall-clock preprocessing time and post-processing feature count — before opening the dashboard session. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lc-msms-feature-count-validation))
- **lc-msms-feature-detection**: Use when raw LC-MS/MS runs in .mzML or .mzXML format from untargeted metabolomics experiments must be converted into features (precursor ion, adduct, isotope peaks) with merged fragmentation spectra per feature before de novo formula and structure annotation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lc-msms-feature-detection))
- **lc-msms-feature-detection-and-alignment**: Use when raw LC-MS/MS files from bioassay-guided fractionation must be converted into a feature-level table aligned across fractions before Pearson correlation scoring and GNPS FBMN. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lc-msms-feature-detection-and-alignment))
- **lc-msms-feature-mz-rt-matching**: Use when a preprocessed margheRita mRList (MS-Dial-exported features with m/z, retention time and MS/MS spectra) must be matched against a reference spectral library to produce feature-metabolite associations up to level-1 confidence. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lc-msms-feature-mz-rt-matching))
- **lc-msms-feature-network-edge-overlay**: Use when a globaltest-prioritized LC-MS/MS differential feature must be inspected in its spectral-similarity network context — e.g. to check whether its strongest neighbors (top-30 edges) reach beyond its small k-medoid cluster. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lc-msms-feature-network-edge-overlay))
- **lc-msms-feature-quantification**: Use when raw LC–MS/MS runs (e.g. mzML/MGF per sample) must be turned into per-feature relative abundances that support bioactivity correlation scoring or fraction-based prioritization of bioactive natural products. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lc-msms-feature-quantification))
- **lc-msms-feature-quantification-table-transposition**: Use when an aligned LC-MS/MS feature table (row ID, row m/z, row retention time, peak area) has been exported and bioassay values per fraction have been added, and feature-wise correlation scoring requires features-as-rows orientation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lc-msms-feature-quantification-table-transposition))
- **lc-msms-feature-set-data-integration**: Use when a completed msFeaST pipeline has produced embedding coordinates, k-medoids cluster labels, and globaltest statistics per feature, and these must be joined on the m/z–retention-time feature key and serialized into the msFeaST_Dashboard_bundle.html-compatible .json schema. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lc-msms-feature-set-data-integration))
- **lc-msms-feature-spectra-preprocessing**: Use when an LC-MS/MS untargeted metabolomics dataset (e.g. a Kathib et al. 2024 feature table with quantification, metadata, and MS/MS spectra) must be prepared for msFeaST spectral similarity computation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lc-msms-feature-spectra-preprocessing))
- **lc-msms-feature-table-filtering**: Use when you have a Progenesis-format LC-MS/MS peak table plus sample metadata and need to correct mispicked isotope peaks, remove solvent-blank/media background, CV-filter technical replicates, or drop in-source fragments. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lc-msms-feature-table-filtering))
- **lc-msms-feature-table-import-progenesis**: Use when you have tandem MS/MS pre-processed feature tables exported in Progenesis format (a peak table CSV plus a sample metadata CSV) and need to load them into R as an mpactr object for quality filtering. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lc-msms-feature-table-import-progenesis))
- **lc-msms-feature-table-ingestion**: Use when starting a margheRita workflow from MS-Dial-exported LC-MS/MS feature tables and sample metadata, before any filtering, normalization, annotation or statistics can run. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lc-msms-feature-table-ingestion))
- **lc-msms-feature-table-processing**: Use when starting from raw LC-MS/MS feature abundance tables, sample group metadata, and MS/MS peak lists (e.g., the Pleurotus eryngii 0% vs 80% olive mill solid waste contrast) and needing a cleaned, msFeaST-ready data bundle. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lc-msms-feature-table-processing))
- **lc-msms-fingerprint-image-generation**: Use when raw untargeted LC-MS/MS metabolomics data (MS1/MS2 m/z–intensity pairs from MS-DIAL) must be encoded as 2D grayscale fingerprint images as CNN input for bioactivity classification without compound annotation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lc-msms-fingerprint-image-generation))
- **lc-msms-ground-truth-evaluation**: Use when you have MS/MS spectra of a compound set whose molecular formulas/structures were validated by spectral library search and manual inspection, and you need to quantify annotation accuracy of a formula- or structure-annotation tool. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lc-msms-ground-truth-evaluation))
- **lc-msms-identification-count-verification**: Use when a paper reports a total unique-metabolite count from DDA LC-MS/MS and an accompanying organ/mode-specific database file (e.g. an .xlsx workbook with per-organ sheets) that must be reconciled against that count. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lc-msms-identification-count-verification))
- **lc-msms-metadata-parsing**: Use when constructing a large unannotated MS/MS corpus from raw metabolomics repository files (MassIVE GNPS .mzML/.mzXML) and you need per-spectrum LC–MS/MS metadata preserved alongside peak lists for downstream ML. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lc-msms-metadata-parsing))
- **lc-msms-mzml-data-parsing**: Use when an mzML file contains MS2 scans from a simulated co-eluting peptide mixture and the goal is per-scan peak extraction plus annotation against theoretical fragment ions to quantify chimeric spectra. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lc-msms-mzml-data-parsing))
- **lc-msms-peak-coverage-evaluation-from-fullscan-data**: Use when the goal is to quantify DDA coverage (proportion of MS1 ions fragmented) for SmartROI or WeightedDEW controller settings by simulating LC-MS/MS injections from full-scan mzML data in ViMMS. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lc-msms-peak-coverage-evaluation-from-fullscan-data))
- **lc-msms-peak-picking-mzmine**: Use when raw full-scan LC-MS/MS mzML files (e.g. serum QCA or beer QCB extract injections) must be converted into picked-peak RT/mz bounding boxes so that DDA controller coverage of picked peaks can be tabulated. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lc-msms-peak-picking-mzmine))
- **lc-msms-simulation-environment-orchestration**: Use when a prepared chemical list (e.g. from ChemicalMixtureCreator) must be subjected to a simulated fragmentation strategy (Top-N DDA, DIA, SmartROI, WeightedDEW) and the resulting scans exported as an mzML file. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lc-msms-simulation-environment-orchestration))
- **lc-msms-spectra-fingerprinting**: Use when comparing chemodiverse metabolomics samples with poor feature overlap, strong retention time shifts, or data acquired across different LC methods or mass spectrometers (Q-ToF vs Orbitrap), where feature alignment is impractical. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lc-msms-spectra-fingerprinting))
- **lc-msms-spectral-clustering**: Use when you have per-sample MGF MS2 spectra from LC–MS/MS feature detection (e.g. MSV000080502) and need to group them into clean consensus spectra, merging duplicates of the same ion yet keeping distinct isomers of the same m/z (e.g. 11 isomers of m/z 589.31) separated. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lc-msms-spectral-clustering))
- **lc-msms-spectral-clustering-parameter-tuning**: Use when an LC-MS/MS dataset (mzML/mzXML/mgf uploaded to MassIVE) must be molecular-networked and the practitioner needs to know how clustering and tolerance settings affect node counts and annotation yield for a compound class of interest. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lc-msms-spectral-clustering-parameter-tuning))
- **lc-msms-spectral-library-matching**: Use when you have an aligned LC-MS/MS feature table (e.g. from MS-Dial after MS-CleanR filtering) and need to annotate detected features by matching their MS/MS spectra against a spectral library. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lc-msms-spectral-library-matching))
- **lc-msms-spectral-preprocessing**: Use when raw LC-MS/MS spectra from paired genome–metabolome samples (e.g. PoDP bacterial/fungal strains or cryptic features from MZmine) must be deconvoluted, aligned, and converted into MS/MS fingerprints for NPOmix KNN linking of spectra to biosynthetic gene clusters. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lc-msms-spectral-preprocessing))
- **lc-msms-untargeted-metabolomics**: Use when analyzing LC-MS/MS untargeted metabolomics feature tables with MS/MS spectra, where the goal is to group features by spectral similarity and statistically prioritize feature groups across sample conditions. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lc-msms-untargeted-metabolomics))
- **lc-msms-untargeted-metabolomics-data-processing**: Use when an agent must reproduce or extend KGMN annotation of known and unknown metabolites from untargeted LC-MS/MS metabolomics output (e.g., NIST human urine positive/negative mode) and quantify how far annotation expands beyond seed metabolites. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lc-msms-untargeted-metabolomics-data-processing))
- **lc-msms-untargeted-metabolomics-processing**: Use when raw LC–MS/MS untargeted metabolomics files (e.g. mzML from a Bruker Impac II qQToF or similar) plus a Metadata Table describing samples/sample groups must be converted into quantified, cleaned consensus spectra, library annotations, and a filtered SSMN. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lc-msms-untargeted-metabolomics-processing))
- **lc-msms-workflow-result-integration**: Use when raw PSM tables from LC-MS/MS peptide searches must be filtered (unique peptides, unique monoisotopic mass, &gt;=5 peptides/protein) to confirm putative MSI identifications or replicate Figure 4-style filtering cascades. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lc-msms-workflow-result-integration))

- **lcm-arrows-report**: Полный цикл LayerCycleManager в AutoCAD — нарисовать стрелки отклонений между двумя циклами, построить ведомость отклонений (LCMDEV) и записать новый блок в книгу Excel. Использовать на просьбы «отрисуй стрелки», «построй ведомость», «заполни таблицу отклонений», «сделай полный цикл». ([mrpatrickstar26/LayerCycleManager](https://github.com/mrpatrickstar26/LayerCycleManager/tree/HEAD/SKILLS/lcm-arrows-report) · ★2)
- **lcm-dogfood**: Exercise a live lcm install (installed plugin, real summarizer, the user's own data) and report a scorecard. Usage: /lcm-dogfood [health|capture|import|compact|sensitive|mcp|integrity] ([lossless-claude/lcm](https://github.com/lossless-claude/lcm/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/lcm-dogfood) · ★60)
- **lcm-memory**: Search and store lcm memory with the lcm CLI. Use before a code task in this project, before asking the user about something a past session may have settled, and when a durable insight (decision, preference, root-cause, pattern, gotcha, solution, workflow) surfaces. ([lossless-claude/lcm](https://github.com/lossless-claude/lcm/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/lcm-memory) · ★60)
- **lcm-recall**: Use this skill when answering a question requires looking up exact historical evidence — past commands, file paths, error messages, decisions, or conversation history — that is no longer in active context. Triggers on phrases like "what did we decide about X", "find where we discussed Y", "look up the error from yesterday", or "what was the exact command we used". The lossless-claude MCP tools (lcm_grep, lcm_describe, lcm_stats) query the shared lossless-claw SQLite database, which holds messages from this Claude Code session, prior compacted CC sessions, and Daphne's full OpenClaw conversati… ([offendingcommit/lossless-claude](https://github.com/offendingcommit/lossless-claude/tree/HEAD/skills/lcm-recall) · ★1)
- **lcme**: LCME accreditation gap analysis for MD programs. Maps all 12 LCME Standards and their ~93 Elements against documentation in the Brain to identify evidential gaps, stale evidence, and site-visit risk. Use when preparing for a full accreditation review, interim report, DCI submission, or any LCME compliance checkpoint. ([Nantasket-AI/he-compliance-brain](https://github.com/Nantasket-AI/he-compliance-brain/tree/HEAD/skills/lcme))
- **lcmp_recommendation**: Suggest LCMP compaction when explicitly requested - provides guidance on promoting durable information to decisions.md, insights.md, and gotchas.md without auto-compaction ([grandinh/claude-chaos-express](https://github.com/grandinh/claude-chaos-express/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/lcmp_recommendation) · ★2)
- **lcms-adduct-annotation**: Use when geoRge's PuInc_seeker and basepeak_finder have produced labeled base-peak m/z values on an XCMS feature table and you need putative metabolite identities with adduct assignments in negative-ion LC/MS. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lcms-adduct-annotation))
- **lcms-alignment-benchmarking**: Use when benchmarking the m/z offset detection accuracy of an LC-HRMS alignment tool by injecting known m/z shifts into a reference feature list and comparing imposed versus detected offsets. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lcms-alignment-benchmarking))
- **lcms-annotation-workflow-execution**: Use when you have a filtered peak list from an LC-MS assay with m/z, RT and adduct/fragment information, and need a KEGG-based, context-prioritized compound annotation per peak rather than a simple mass matching. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lcms-annotation-workflow-execution))
- **lcms-compound-spectra-extraction**: Use when you have an XCMS feature table from UPLC/ESI-QTOFMS data (positive and negative ion mode) and need to group co-eluting, adduct, and isotope features into per-analyte compound spectra before compound identification. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lcms-compound-spectra-extraction))
- **lcms-cross-dataset-feature-matching**: Use when two or more independently processed same-method nontargeted LC-MS datasets must be aligned to form a complete dataset, and you need pairwise Source→Target subalignment match tables. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lcms-cross-dataset-feature-matching))
- **lcms-data-format-handling**: Use when you have raw LC-MS data from a vendor instrument or in netCDF format and need to ingest it into SLAW or similar untargeted LC-MS workflows. Use this skill when raw data must be converted to mzML, validated for centroiding and polarity uniformity, and prepared for peak-picking dispatch. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/lcms-data-format-handling) · 2 copies)
- **lcms-data-format-parsing**: Use when when you have raw LC/MS data in mzML format and need to execute the LAGF non-targeted screening pipeline. Use this skill as the first step before applying the LAGF algorithm workflow to extract and annotate features from mass spectrometry data. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/lcms-data-format-parsing) · 2 copies)
- **lcms-data-ingestion**: Use when a Thermo .raw or .mzML LC-MS data file needs to be loaded into lcmsWorld for 3D visualization, especially as the first load of a file that has no cached .lcms conversion yet. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lcms-data-ingestion))
- **lcms-data-quality-control-assessment**: Use when running or monitoring large LC-MS/MS untargeted metabolomics analyses (e.g. Thermo Orbitrap acquisition sequences) and you need per-specimen QC status (Pass/Fail/Warn) for internal standards within minutes of each raw file being written. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lcms-data-quality-control-assessment))
- **lcms-demo-data-loading**: Use when validating or demonstrating MetaboReport end-to-end without user-supplied data — e.g. reproducing the File S5 example HTML report or testing that preprocessing, statistics, visualisation and reporting modules communicate via new_app_state(). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lcms-demo-data-loading))
- **lcms-eic-extraction**: Use when building an in-house LC-MS metabolite standard reference library from mzML files and per-adduct EICs must be generated at expected m/z values before peak finding, peak-quality filtering, or RT assignment. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lcms-eic-extraction--e5fd4873b752d838))
- **lcms-eic-extraction**: Use when raw LC/MS mzML files and a generic peak list (mz, rt columns, e.g. from XCMS) must be converted into per-peak EIC matrices to feed peak-classification or reintegration models. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lcms-eic-extraction--ef8fde397ffb28d7))
- **lcms-extracted-ion-chromatogram-metric-implementation**: Use when you have LC/MS or UPLC/MS profile intensity profiles (extracted ion chromatograms) and need to score each EIC or detected peak's quality with quantitative metrics, or to filter out high-background / single-peak / spike / zigzag noise before time-consuming peak detection processing. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lcms-extracted-ion-chromatogram-metric-implementation))
- **lcms-feature-alignment-across-datasets**: Use when two or more independently processed, same-method nontargeted LC-MS feature datasets — each a merged Feature Metadata + Feature Abundances table — must be combined into one aligned feature set with overlapping features matched. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lcms-feature-alignment-across-datasets))
- **lcms-feature-alignment-evaluation**: Use when benchmarking feature alignment methods (e.g. G-Aligner, MZmine2, OpenMS, XCMS) on mzML-derived feature lists against a manually annotated ground truth of library analytes. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lcms-feature-alignment-evaluation))
- **lcms-feature-annotation**: Use when a peak table from untargeted LC-MS metabolomics needs putative compound-level annotations from m/z values, especially when candidates must be prioritized against KEGG context networks rather than by m/z match alone. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lcms-feature-annotation))
- **lcms-feature-annotation-against-hmdb**: Use when a MultiABLER-generated feature table of metabolomic or lipidomic peaks needs compound-level annotation and lipid-versus-metabolite labels before downstream statistical analysis such as sPLS or rCCA. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lcms-feature-annotation-against-hmdb))
- **lcms-feature-annotation-table-generation**: Use when each sample has a minimal metadata file with its originating taxon plus MZmine-processed LC-MS/MS DDA feature MS2 spectra, and you need per-feature structural annotations reranked by taxonomy and chemical consistency. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lcms-feature-annotation-table-generation))
- **lcms-feature-classification**: Use when you have an LCMS feature table annotated with MS2 spectral data and need to distinguish in-source fragments (mass loss patterns, same retention time, MS2 spectral relationships) from true metabolite features. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/lcms-feature-classification) · 2 copies)
- **lcms-feature-correlation-clustering**: Use when a preprocessed LC-MS feature abundance matrix contains multiple features per metabolite (adducts, isotopes) that need grouping, or when redundant correlated features must be collapsed before statistical analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lcms-feature-correlation-clustering))
- **lcms-feature-correspondence-matching-across-tools**: Use when benchmarking feature detection/quantification of LC-MS processing tools on a dilution or mixing-ratio dataset, where feature tables from multiple tools must be matched and ranked by correlation of peak areas to known abundances. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lcms-feature-correspondence-matching-across-tools))
- **lcms-feature-correspondence-metrics-computation**: Use when two LC–MS metabolomics feature tables have been matched (e.g. via M2S) and you need a quantitative validation of match quality — fraction of correctly matched annotated features, counts of wrong matches, and annotated features outside thresholds. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lcms-feature-correspondence-metrics-computation))
- **lcms-feature-detection-and-alignment**: Use when you have raw centroided LC-MS runs (mzXML/mzML) from a spatially mapped sample set and need a per-run feature table aligned across all runs to build intensity maps or heat maps. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lcms-feature-detection-and-alignment))
- **lcms-feature-detection-and-quantification**: Use when you have raw LC-MS data (mzML or equivalent format) from a metabolomics experiment and need to extract a reproducible, quantified feature table with intensity measurements before conducting metabolite identification or statistical analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/lcms-feature-detection-and-quantification) · 3 copies)
- **lcms-feature-detection-and-quantification**: Use when raw LC-MS/MS untargeted metabolomics data (Thermo RAW, Agilent, Bruker, or mzML files) must be converted into a table of metabolic features with alignment, adduct annotation, and quantification, and you need benchmarkable detection/quantification rates. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lcms-feature-detection-and-quantification))
- **lcms-feature-detection-benchmarking**: Use when multiple LC-MS data processing tools must be compared for feature detection consistency, or when reported feature counts (e.g. 10,901 XCMS vs 42,099 MZmine features on 184 pooled HZV029 plasma samples) need to be reproduced. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lcms-feature-detection-benchmarking))
- **lcms-feature-detection-parameterization**: Use when processing raw LC-MS/MS DDA metabolomics files (positive and/or negative ionization) with MZmine in a sample-centric workflow, where parameterization and feature filtering must be fixed before RDF/KG integration. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lcms-feature-detection-parameterization))
- **lcms-feature-extraction**: Use when when you have raw LC-MS chromatographic data (mzML or vendor format) and need to identify and characterize all detectable peaks across the full retention time range for untargeted metabolomics or discovery workflows. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/lcms-feature-extraction) · 2 copies)
- **lcms-feature-extraction-parameterization**: Use when raw LC-MS-based metabolomics data must be processed for feature extraction and default software parameters underperform, or when you need data-driven values for mass tolerance, peak height, peak width, or instrumental shift. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lcms-feature-extraction-parameterization))
- **lcms-feature-filtering**: Use when an MZmine-exported full-scan feature table (.csv with row ID, row m/z, row retention time, per-sample peak areas) must be reduced to biologically consistent ions before MS/MS targeted acquisition. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lcms-feature-filtering))
- **lcms-feature-grouping**: Use when you have a feature table from LC-MS untargeted metabolomics or stable isotope tracing and need to group degenerate ions (isotopes/adducts) into empirical compounds with inferred neutral masses, without prior formula knowledge. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lcms-feature-grouping))
- **lcms-feature-grouping-graph-partitioning**: Use when an xsAnnotate object has already been grouped by retention time (groupFWHM) and isotopes (findIsotopes), but compound spectra may still contain features of two or more closely co-eluting compounds. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lcms-feature-grouping-graph-partitioning))
- **lcms-feature-grouping-retention-time-coelution**: Use when processing raw LC/MS feature lists (e.g. from XCMS) where multiple m/z features originate from a single metabolite and you need to reduce feature-list redundancy before adduct or isotope annotation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lcms-feature-grouping-retention-time-coelution))
- **lcms-feature-intensity-ratio-computation**: Use when XCMS peak-picked LC/MS data contains labeled and unlabeled biologically equivalent samples (e.g., 12C vs 13C glucose) and you need fold-change intensities of enriched features versus a control condition. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lcms-feature-intensity-ratio-computation))
- **lcms-feature-intensity-rsd-calculation**: Use when an aligned LC-MS feature table with replicate sample-intensity columns and a 'level' column (PP/MR) is available, and quantitative precision of rescued MR features must be benchmarked against conventional peak-picked PP features. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lcms-feature-intensity-rsd-calculation))
- **lcms-feature-intensity-ttesting**: Use when you have a feature-intensity matrix from LC/MS preprocessing (apLCMS or XCMS) plus batch and phenotype labels, and need to test each feature for between-group differences after batch-effect correction. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lcms-feature-intensity-ttesting))
- **lcms-feature-list-perturbation**: Use when validating or calibrating an LC-HRMS feature-matching/alignment tool where ground truth is unknown: perturb a processed reference feature list (m/z + retention time) with known shifts and score recovery (F-measure, ppm discrepancy). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lcms-feature-list-perturbation))
- **lcms-feature-matching**: Use when integrating two similar untargeted LC–MS metabolomics feature tables (e.g. two batches or two cohorts peak-picked with XCMS) that need feature-level correspondence based solely on RT, MZ, and FI values. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lcms-feature-matching))
- **lcms-feature-matrix-construction**: Use when raw LC-MS mass spectra must be converted into a fixed-dimension 2D feature matrix (m/z bins × RT windows) for feeding CNN/LSTM/Transformer classifiers, and spectrum_dim differs per dataset (e.g. 12000 NSCLC/CRLM, 9900 RCC, 15000 Canine Sarcoma). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lcms-feature-matrix-construction))
- **lcms-feature-pair-alignment-reduction**: Use when metabCombiner has produced a scored combined table after fit_gam and calcScores, and conflicting or low-scoring FPAs must be pruned to form the final reduced table before downstream statistics. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lcms-feature-pair-alignment-reduction))
- **lcms-feature-pair-screening-deuterium-labelling**: Use when LC/MS peak data from a stable-isotope feeding experiment (processed with XCMS and CAMERA) must be screened for label-shifted feature pairs to enumerate putative metabolites of the fed precursor. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lcms-feature-pair-screening-deuterium-labelling))
- **lcms-feature-percentage-computation**: Use when analyzing how capillary voltage, ion energy, or collision energy settings change the number and percentage of ISF features detected relative to real metabolic features in LC-MS feature tables. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lcms-feature-percentage-computation))
- **lcms-feature-probability-scoring**: Use when you have an untargeted LC/MS feature table (m/z, retention time, intensity columns) and need to move beyond single-hit matching to probabilistic annotation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/lcms-feature-probability-scoring) · 2 copies)
- **lcms-feature-quality-filtering**: Use when an extracted LC–MS feature matrix (mzML-derived peaks, TidyMS Data Container, MZmine/XCMS output) needs cleaning before statistical analysis and you have pooled/intrastudy QC, blank, and study samples to drive reproducibility and background filters. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lcms-feature-quality-filtering))
- **lcms-feature-quantification-peak-area-extraction**: Use when LC-MS data contain a dilution/mixing series with known composition ratios (e.g. BM21 plasma–vegetable juice dilutions) and you need to compare peak-area quantification quality across processing tools like asari vs XCMS. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lcms-feature-quantification-peak-area-extraction))
- **lcms-feature-relationship-export**: Use when after ISFrag has completed identification of in-source fragment features (Part 4 output), when you need to serialize and inspect the hierarchical fragmentation relationships among identified ISF features, or when preparing data for visualization or external analysis of fragment lineage and. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/lcms-feature-relationship-export) · 2 copies)
- **lcms-feature-rescue-from-dda-in-dia**: Use when DIA (SWATH) feature tables of individual biological samples lack metabolic features that were confidently detected in the pooled-QC DDA run, and their intensities must be recovered from the DIA raw data. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lcms-feature-rescue-from-dda-in-dia))
- **lcms-feature-table-analysis**: Use when processing LC/MS feature tables from untargeted metabolomics where features must be grouped into compound spectra and annotated with ion species to cut feature count before statistical analysis or compound identification. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lcms-feature-table-analysis))
- **lcms-feature-table-annotation**: Use when apLCMS- or XCMS-generated feature tables (m/z, RT, intensity columns) from LC/MS metabolomics data acquired in negative ion mode need KEGG metabolite annotation via adduct matching, e.g. to compare annotation/matching rates across preprocessing methods. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lcms-feature-table-annotation--0e87a6d0ed8c7e3e))
- **lcms-feature-table-annotation**: Use when you have a tab-delimited feature table (feature ID, m/z, rtime, intensities) from LC-MS data — e.g. from asari v1.9.2 — and need to group degenerate ions into empirical compounds and infer neutral masses before database annotation, including stable isotope tracing. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lcms-feature-table-annotation--09a0db8b0f3523a4))
- **lcms-feature-table-construction**: Use when you have centroided, single-polarity mzML files from DDA LC-MS experiments and need to generate a quantitative feature table with aligned m/z and retention time coordinates, isotopic annotations, and MS2 spectra for downstream statistical or annotation analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/lcms-feature-table-construction) · 2 copies)
- **lcms-feature-table-filtering**: Use when an LC/MS untargeted metabolomics feature table must be turned into the two input lists (significant m/z features and reference list of all detected features) that mummichog requires for pathway/network activity prediction. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lcms-feature-table-filtering--14a2cde736d2dabd))
- **lcms-feature-table-filtering**: Use when a feature table with mz, rt, and group-defined intensity columns is available and features enriched between two conditions (e.g. hacl-1 vs WT) must be isolated with intensity, significance, and fold-change cutoffs. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lcms-feature-table-filtering--4c50bbbee26bf3d9))
- **lcms-feature-table-import**: Use when you have raw mzML files plus an mzMine- or XCMS-derived feature table CSV/TSV and need to bring both into a single NeatMS Experiment for downstream peak labelling. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lcms-feature-table-import))
- **lcms-feature-table-interpretation**: Use when you have an LC-MS feature table (from XCMS mzXML extraction or a custom csv from MS-DIAL/MZmine2) and need to identify which features are in-source fragments of which precursor ions. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lcms-feature-table-interpretation))
- **lcms-feature-table-parsing**: Use when feature tables from multiple LC-MS processing tools must be parsed and unified for cross-tool comparison or per-feature rescoring, e.g. recomputing peak-shape fitness scores on the Yeast2021 dataset. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lcms-feature-table-parsing))
- **lcms-feature-table-parsing**: Use when you have raw nontargeted LCMS feature tables from one or more analytical methods in tabular format (with m/z, RT, and intensity columns) that need to be aligned or clustered, or when integrating multiple feature tables into a shared BMXP processing pipeline that requires standardized. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/lcms-feature-table-parsing) · 3 copies)
- **lcms-feature-table-processing**: Use when raw LC-MS(/MS) files from a serially diluted sample set must be preprocessed in MZmine into aligned full-scan feature tables (.csv with row ID, row m/z, row retention time, peak areas) suitable for downstream regression-based feature selection. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lcms-feature-table-processing))
- **lcms-feature-to-compound-spectrum-assignment**: Reference for grouping LC/MS peaks detected by XCMS into per-analyte compound spectra when you need to de-redundant a feature list and know which features co-elute with a compound. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lcms-feature-to-compound-spectrum-assignment))
- **lcms-feature-to-metabolite-mapping**: Use when a cleaned LC-MS feature table (mass_dataset object) needs metabolite annotations, and database-level identifications (MSI level 2) must be produced by matching m/z and retention time. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lcms-feature-to-metabolite-mapping))
- **lcms-file-level-quality-filtering**: Use when building training corpora from repository-scale LC-MS/MS deposits (e.g. MassIVE GNPS .mzML/.mzXML experiments) and you need to drop low-quality files before spectrum-level QC. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lcms-file-level-quality-filtering))
- **lcms-glycan-identification-reporting**: Use when the goal is to identify fluorescently labeled N-linked glycans from LC-MS/MS data acquired on an Orbitrap (or MALDI-TOF) instrument, replacing manual analyst peak picking with automated MS1/MS2 searching and Excel reporting. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lcms-glycan-identification-reporting))
- **lcms-homologue-series-detection**: Use when a pre-processed LC-MS peak table (m/z, rt, intensity columns, e.g. the mtbls1358 dataset) should be annotated with homologue series IDs — e.g. to find polymer/PEG-like repeating-unit ladders or compare targeted vs untargeted detection runs. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lcms-homologue-series-detection))
- **lcms-injection-list-formatting**: Use when preparing flow-injection MSn acquisition for a Thermo Orbitrap ID-X and you have plate-based metadata (unique sample id, plate_id, well_location) that must become an Xcalibur import sequence. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lcms-injection-list-formatting))
- **lcms-intensity-normalization-comparison**: Use when an LC/MS ion-feature matrix (&gt;150 samples) has QC injections that can be withheld, and the goal is to test whether data-driven synthetic-QC drift correction matches experimental-QC normalization. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lcms-intensity-normalization-comparison))
- **lcms-intensity-rank-sorting**: Use when processing raw LC-MS-based metabolomics data in R to derive universal feature-extraction parameters via rank-ordered intensity profiles and zone-of-interest structures, prior to tools like MS-DIAL or XCMS CentWave. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lcms-intensity-rank-sorting))
- **lcms-isotope-label-detection**: Use when you have XCMS-processed untargeted LC/MS feature data from paired unlabeled vs. labeled samples (e.g. CELL_Glc12 vs CELL_Glc13) and need to flag isotopically enriched features and their base-peak isotope assignments. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lcms-isotope-label-detection))
- **lcms-isotope-pattern-mapping**: Use when an LC-MS untargeted feature table (ids, rel.ids, mzs, RTs, Ints) has been feature-clustered and isotope peaks must be grouped into isotope-pattern units before IPA annotation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lcms-isotope-pattern-mapping))
- **lcms-isotopologue-pattern-detection**: Use when you have a table of significant features whose column headers encode AssayName_RTsec_m/z, and you need to group isotopologues and adducts into structural clusters across multi-assay LC-MS metabolomics data. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lcms-isotopologue-pattern-detection))
- **lcms-lipidomics-data-reprocessing**: Use when reprocessing publicly deposited LC-MS lipidomics raw files (Thermo .raw, e.g. MSV000091033 Nile rat plasma) to reproduce or re-annotate reported lipid feature counts and identifications. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lcms-lipidomics-data-reprocessing))
- **lcms-mass-track-extraction-from-mzml**: Use when centroided mzML LC-MS files must be converted into per-sample mass tracks (full-RT intensity vectors per unique m/z) and aligned into a MassGrid before peak picking or annotation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lcms-mass-track-extraction-from-mzml))
- **lcms-metabolite-detection-counting**: Use when a study has a list of predicted metabolites from a tool like BioTransformer and an LC-HRMS detection list from postincubation samples, and the goal is to compute the number (or percentage) of predictions that were actually observed. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lcms-metabolite-detection-counting))
- **lcms-metabolite-library-benchmarking**: Use when quantifying the computational and manual effort of an LC-MS metabolite library construction workflow (e.g., metScribeR on mzML standard files) against a manual peak-picking baseline, to report per-standard effort and total time savings. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lcms-metabolite-library-benchmarking))
- **lcms-metabolomics-data-preprocessing**: Use when an LC-MS metabolomics feature table (XCMS/MZmine2/SIEVE/Skyline output) with pooled QC samples injected at fixed intervals shows injection-order signal drift and needs drift correction before statistical analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lcms-metabolomics-data-preprocessing))
- **lcms-metabolomics-data-processing**: Use when you have raw LC-HRMS metabolomics data in .mzML or . ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/lcms-metabolomics-data-processing) · 3 copies)
- **lcms-metadata-table-curation**: Use when preparing a GNPS molecular networking (METABOLOMICS-SNETS-V2) or library-search job from LC-MS/MS data and a sample metadata table must be collated, standardized, and attached so the analysis is publishable and reproducible. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lcms-metadata-table-curation))
- **lcms-missing-value-imputation**: Use when a non-targeted LC-MS peak table stored as a notame MetaboSet still contains NA values in its exprs matrix after drift correction, quality flagging, and QC removal, and those gaps must be filled before statistical analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lcms-missing-value-imputation))
- **lcms-ms-map-image-conversion**: Use when raw LC–MS data (m/z vs retention time) must be converted into NPFimg's MS-map image format to enable marker feature identification and produce a marker feature table without peak picking. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lcms-ms-map-image-conversion))
- **lcms-ms1-bayesian-annotation**: Use when a clustered MS1 feature table (intensities across samples) from LC-MS untargeted metabolomics needs compound and adduct annotations with statistically rigorous probability estimates, especially when MS2 spectra or prior experiment knowledge should be integrated. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lcms-ms1-bayesian-annotation))
- **lcms-ms1-signal-detection**: Use when you have raw LC/MS untargeted metabolomics data in mzML format (e.g., HILIC positive-mode serum studies) and need to detect metabolite signals without user-set parameters, QC samples, or manual threshold tuning. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lcms-ms1-signal-detection))
- **lcms-ms1-signal-matching-across-scans**: Use when starting nontargeted LC-MS feature detection from raw MS1 scans (mzML or Bruker timsTOF .d) and you need 100% signal coverage rather than peak-shape-filtered peak picking. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lcms-ms1-signal-matching-across-scans))
- **lcms-multi-dataset-alignment**: Use when two or more nontargeted LC-MS feature tables (Feature Metadata + Feature Abundances) from independently processed but same-method datasets must be merged into one complete dataset, and existing pairwise alignment tools do not fully meet the need. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lcms-multi-dataset-alignment))
- **lcms-noise-and-background-modeling**: Use when EICs extracted from LC/MS profile data (e.g. Agilent HPLC Q-TOF metabolomics runs) show high background signals or contain only a single peak, and a quality score is needed to filter bad EICs prior to time-consuming peak detection. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lcms-noise-and-background-modeling))
- **lcms-peak-alignment-and-annotation**: Use when you have an XCMS-processed feature set (XCMSet object) from replicate LC/MS runs comparing labeled (e.g., 13C-glucose) and unlabeled (e.g., 12C-glucose) conditions, and need to identify which features show isotope incorporation (fold-change ≥1.5, p-value &lt;0. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/lcms-peak-alignment-and-annotation) · 2 copies)
- **lcms-peak-annotation-prioritization**: Use when you have a filtered LC-MS peak table (signal-bearing features, 80% rule applied) and need a biologically prioritized KEGG compound annotation list that outperforms mass-only matching, e.g. reproducing mWISE vs xMSannotator sensitivity benchmarks. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lcms-peak-annotation-prioritization))
- **lcms-peak-artifact-classification**: Use when raw mzML files and an XCMS peak list need peak-level curation — i.e., detected peaks must be scored as artifacts vs. true peaks with minimal manual labeling (e.g., &lt;100 peaks) before downstream statistics. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lcms-peak-artifact-classification))
- **lcms-peak-classification-metrics-comparison**: Use when evaluating whether a machine learning peak filter (deep neural network or logistic regression) removes false LC-MS peaks without losing true positives, by comparing ROC curves, AUC, TPR/FPR, and retained false peaks across training, test, and independent cohorts. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lcms-peak-classification-metrics-comparison))
- **lcms-peak-classification-scoring**: Use when nontargeted LC-MS peak lists (e.g. from MZmine 2 centWave extraction) contain a large fraction of false noise peaks and you need to classify and filter peak groups using peak-shape information. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lcms-peak-classification-scoring))
- **lcms-peak-curation**: Use when you have raw mzML files plus an XCMS-generated peak list (m/z and rt columns) and need to remove artifact peaks — e.g. replicate, blank, or spiked-standard metabolomics data where peak quality determines downstream statistics. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lcms-peak-curation))
- **lcms-peak-detection-and-alignment**: Use when you have one or more raw mzXML or mzML LCMS data files (from DDA, DIA, or fullscan acquisition) and need to extract quantitative metabolite features for multi-sample comparative analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/lcms-peak-detection-and-alignment) · 2 copies)
- **lcms-peak-detection-parameterization**: Use when raw LC-MS/MS data (converted to mzML) from a Multi-ABLE extraction must be converted into a processed, aligned peak table with adduct annotations before downstream filtering, normalization, or joint metabolomic–lipidomic analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lcms-peak-detection-parameterization))
- **lcms-peak-detection-preprocessing**: Use when raw LC/MS profile data (e.g. NetCDF/CDF files from Metabolomics Workbench) were acquired in multiple batches and consistent feature detection/quantification across batches is required before downstream analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lcms-peak-detection-preprocessing))
- **lcms-peak-feature-extraction**: Use when raw nontargeted LC-MS metabolomics data (e.g., human plasma HILIC runs) must be converted into peak lists/peak groups before ML-based true vs false peak filtering. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lcms-peak-feature-extraction))
- **lcms-peak-filling**: Use when an XCMS xcmsSet has missing peaks after grouping and you need filled feature intensities, especially for features flagged misaligned by ncGTW, to compare CV before vs after realignment. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lcms-peak-filling))
- **lcms-peak-image-rasterization**: Use when nontargeted LC-MS peak lists from MZmine 2 must be converted into fixed-size 64x64 raster images so a deep neural network can classify candidate peaks as true or false. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lcms-peak-image-rasterization))
- **lcms-peak-integration-evaluation**: Use when targeted LC-MS data (e.g., HRMS full-scan or SRM runs from instruments like the Waters Synapt G2 HDMS) must have known small-molecule targets extracted as ion chromatograms, peak-picked, and integrated for quantification or between-group comparison. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lcms-peak-integration-evaluation))
- **lcms-peak-intensity-filtering**: Use when after XCMS peak picking, alignment, and grouping when you have identified putative incorporations (via PuInc_seeker) or base-peak isotopologue candidates and need to exclude low-intensity peaks that are likely noise or instrument artifacts. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/lcms-peak-intensity-filtering) · 2 copies)
- **lcms-peak-list-alignment**: Use when a set of peak lists from multiple LC-MS samples (e.g., 20 synthetic lipidomic samples derived from a seed sample) must be merged into an aligned peak list, especially when random non-linear RT shifts are present. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lcms-peak-list-alignment))
- **lcms-peak-quality-classification**: Use when unaligned or aligned mzMine2/XCMS feature tables must be labelled and filtered to remove false-positive MS1 peaks before downstream statistical analysis of large-scale LC-MS metabolomics data. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lcms-peak-quality-classification))
- **lcms-peak-quality-feature-engineering**: Use when detected chromatographic peaks from LC/MS metabolomics data must be classified as good or bad, and single-metric cutoff filtering is insufficient — combine multiple peak quality metrics as ML features. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lcms-peak-quality-feature-engineering))
- **lcms-peak-table-automated-transfer**: Use when running the Nextflow4MS-DIAL pipeline on .mzML LC-HRMS input and you need MS-DIAL's peak table to flow into MS-FLO automatically without manual file copying or manual re-parameterization between the two tools. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lcms-peak-table-automated-transfer))
- **lcms-peak-table-generation**: Use when starting from raw .mzML (or .abf) LC-HRMS metabolomics files and you need a reproducible, containerized peak-detection/alignment pipeline that yields a filtered MS-DIAL plus MS-FLO peak table (.tsv). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lcms-peak-table-generation))
- **lcms-peptide-mass-table-parsing**: Use when a MALDI-to-LC-MS matching step needs a reference table of LC-MS/MS peptide masses (e.g., peptides.txt) and the table must be read and formatted in R before tolerance-based mass correlation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-proteomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-proteomics/tree/HEAD/collections/proteomics/v1/leaves/lcms-peptide-mass-table-parsing))
- **lcms-processing-software-benchmarking**: Use when comparing the computational efficiency (CPU time, memory, scaling with sample number) of LC-MS metabolomics processing tools on centroided mzML datasets, or when validating claims that a new tool scales better than established ones like XCMS. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lcms-processing-software-benchmarking))
- **lcms-qc-normalization-benchmarking**: Use when comparing the computational cost of QC sample-based normalization methods on targeted (e.g., 53-compound) vs untargeted (e.g., 8913-feature) metabolomics matrices, or when deciding which normalization method is feasible for high-throughput use. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lcms-qc-normalization-benchmarking))
- **lcms-quality-metric-interpretation**: Use when targeted LC–MS peak integration output must be quality-filtered: peaks carry computed metrics (AUC, max intensity, POP, SNR, bell-curve similarity) and 'Good'/'Bad'/'Ambiguous' calls must be evaluated or reproduced. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lcms-quality-metric-interpretation))
- **lcms-raw-data-autotuning-benchmark**: Use when raw LC-MS metabolomics data must be processed and you need to decide which auto-tuning method (Paramounter, AutoTuner, or IPO) yields the most true positive features before committing to a peak-picking parameter set. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lcms-raw-data-autotuning-benchmark))
- **lcms-raw-data-import**: Use when raw vendor or open-format LC-MS files need to be loaded into MZmine 2 before peak picking, or when designing a processing pipeline that must import MS data without precision loss from resampling. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lcms-raw-data-import))
- **lcms-raw-data-import**: Use when you have raw LC/MS data in mzML or mzXML format and need to initiate untargeted metabolomics analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/lcms-raw-data-import) · 2 copies)
- **lcms-raw-data-parameter-measurement**: Use when starting an untargeted LC-MS metabolomics workflow with raw spectra and you need data-driven values for mass tolerance, peak height, peak width, or instrumental shift instead of relying on vendor defaults or manual tuning. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lcms-raw-data-parameter-measurement))
- **lcms-raw-data-reading-mzml**: Use when you have raw LC-MS mzML files (e.g., pooled/intrastudy QC, blanks, and study plasma samples) that must be ingested by TidyMS to extract spectral features for a quality-control or data-curation pipeline. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lcms-raw-data-reading-mzml))
- **lcms-raw-file-format-conversion**: Use when raw LC-MS/MS files (e.g. Orbitrap .raw) must be converted to mzXML so the GlyKAn AZ app's MS1 Peak ID / MS2 Peak Confirmation tabs can ingest them. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lcms-raw-file-format-conversion))
- **lcms-raw-spectra-preprocessing-to-npy**: Use when starting from raw LC-MS metabolomics files (e.g. mzML from MetaboLights studies such as MTBLS1129) that must be auto-converted to .npy arrays for end-to-end deep-learning classification with DeepMSProfiler. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lcms-raw-spectra-preprocessing-to-npy))
- **lcms-replicate-retention-time-analysis**: Use when you have three or more replicate mzXML/mzML injections of a chemical-standard mixture (e.g. from comparing HILIC LC-MS mobile phases) and need per-peak quality metrics, validated peaks, and replicate-consistent retention times. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lcms-replicate-retention-time-analysis))
- **lcms-result-table-structure**: Use when after completing Part 4 (Identification of ISF Features) in the ISFrag workflow, when you have an analysis results object containing identified ISF features and need to generate a shareable, tabular export that documents feature annotations, hierarchical parent–fragment relationships, and.
